<script>
	    var ip = 'null';
	    var countryA2Code = 'null';
	    if(ip==null || ip==undefined || ip=="null" || ip==""){
	
	        
	
	        $.getJSON('https://ipapi.co/json/', function(data) {
	            var detailsFound = false;
	            var ip = '';
	            var a2Code = '';
	            try{
	                //console.log('first try: ', data);
	                ip = data.ip;
	                a2Code = data.country;
	                if(isNotBlank(ip) && isNotBlank(a2Code) && a2Code.length==2){
	                    detailsFound = true;
	                }
	            } catch(e){
	                detailsFound = false;
	            }
	            if(detailsFound){
	                saveIpIntoSession(ip, a2Code, data);
	            } else {
	                //------------------------- second try -------------------
                    // https://freegeoip.net/json/ : deprecated
	                $.getJSON('http://api.ipstack.com/check?access_key=4c8c99b4a745eceb10961eef2cc67e17', function(data) {
	                    try{
	                        //console.log('Second try: ', data);
	                        ip = data.ip;
	                        a2Code = data.country_code;
	                        if(isNotBlank(ip) && isNotBlank(a2Code) && a2Code.length==2){
	                            detailsFound = true;
	                        }
	                    } catch(e){
	                        detailsFound = false;
	                    }
	
	                    if(detailsFound){
	                        saveIpIntoSession(ip, a2Code, data);
	                    } else {
	                        console.log('cant fetch IP_ADD');
	                    }
	                });
	                //----------------------------------------------------- END
	            }
	        });
	
	        var saveIpIntoSession = function(ipAddress, countryA2Code, ipResponseJson){
                var referrerUrl =  document.referrer;
	            var url = getAppContext()+'setIpDtl.html?ip='+ipAddress+'&countryA2Code='+countryA2Code+'&referrer='+referrerUrl;

                var form_data = new FormData();
                form_data.append("ip", ipAddress);
                form_data.append("countryA2Code",countryA2Code);
                form_data.append("referrer",referrerUrl);
                form_data.append("ipResponseJson",JSON.stringify(ipResponseJson));

	            /*$.get(url, function(data, status){
	                console.log('IP ADDED TO SESSION.');
                    localStorage.setItem("ip", ipAddress);
	            });*/

                // Note : In case of API change, change code at java side which fetch country, city name etc etc
                // UserLoginIPService -> saveIPDetail() API side

                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    url: getAppContext()+'setIpDtl.html',
                    //dataType:'text',
                    data: form_data,
                    cache: false,
                    processData: false,
                    contentType: false,
                    error: function(jqXHR, textStatus, errorMessage) {
                        console.log(errorMessage);
                    },
                    success: function(data) {
                        console.log('IP ADDED TO SESSION.');
                        localStorage.setItem("ip", ipAddress);
                    }
                });
	        };
	    } else {
	        console.log('IP Already found, ip:',ip);
	        console.log('countryA2Code', countryA2Code);
	    }
